CLASS ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC IMPLEMENTATION.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->/IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~CREATE_DEEP_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->/IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~COMMIT_WORK
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_RFC_DEST                    TYPE        /IWBEP/DEFI_RFC_DEST(optional)
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~COMMIT_WORK.
* Call RFC commit work functionality
DATA lt_message      TYPE bapiret2. "#EC NEEDED
DATA lv_message_text TYPE BAPI_MSG.
DATA lo_logger       TYPE REF TO /iwbep/cl_cos_logger.
DATA lv_subrc        TYPE syst-subrc.

lo_logger = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_logger( ).

  IF iv_rfc_dest IS INITIAL OR iv_rfc_dest EQ 'NONE'.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
      EXPORTING
      wait   = abap_true
    IMPORTING
      return = lt_message.
  ELSE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
      DESTINATION iv_rfc_dest
    EXPORTING
      wait                  = abap_true
    IMPORTING
      return                = lt_message
    EXCEPTIONS
      communication_failure = 1000 MESSAGE lv_message_text
      system_failure        = 1001 MESSAGE lv_message_text
      OTHERS                = 1002.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
    /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>rfc_exception_handling(
        EXPORTING
          iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
          iv_exp_message_text = lv_message_text
          io_logger           = lo_logger ).
  ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->/IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~LOG_MESSAGE
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_MSG_TYPE                    TYPE        SYMSGTY
* | [--->] IV_MSG_ID                      TYPE        SYMSGID(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MSG_NUMBER                  TYPE        SYMSGNO(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MSG_V1                      TYPE        ANY(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MSG_V2                      TYPE        ANY(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MSG_V3                      TYPE        ANY(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MSG_V4                      TYPE        ANY(optional)
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~LOG_MESSAGE.
* Log message in the application log
DATA lo_logger TYPE REF TO /iwbep/cl_cos_logger.
DATA lv_text TYPE /iwbep/sup_msg_longtext.

  MESSAGE ID iv_msg_id TYPE iv_msg_type NUMBER iv_msg_number
    WITH iv_msg_v1 iv_msg_v2 iv_msg_v3 iv_msg_v4 INTO lv_text.

  lo_logger = mo_context->get_logger( ).
  lo_logger->log_message(
    EXPORTING
     iv_msg_type   = iv_msg_type
     iv_msg_id     = iv_msg_id
     iv_msg_number = iv_msg_number
     iv_msg_text   = lv_text
     iv_msg_v1     = iv_msg_v1
     iv_msg_v2     = iv_msg_v2
     iv_msg_v3     = iv_msg_v3
     iv_msg_v4     = iv_msg_v4
     iv_agent      = 'DPC' ).
  endmethod.

  method /IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~RFC_EXCEPTION_HANDLING.
* RFC call exception handling
DATA lo_logger  TYPE REF TO /iwbep/cl_cos_logger.

lo_logger = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_logger( ).

/iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>rfc_exception_handling(
  EXPORTING
    iv_subrc            = iv_subrc
    iv_exp_message_text = iv_exp_message_text
    io_logger           = lo_logger ).
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~SET_INJECTION.
* Unit test injection
  IF io_unit IS BOUND.
    mo_injection = io_unit.
  ELSE.
    mo_injection = me.
  ENDIF.
  endmethod.

 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.

 DATA ls_iv_imp_exp_type LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_imp_exp_type.
 DATA lr_iv_permit LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_permit.
 DATA ls_iv_permit LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_permit.
 DATA lr_iv_registered_emirates LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_registered_emirates.
 DATA ls_iv_registered_emirates LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_registered_emirates.
 DATA lr_iv_trade_license LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_trade_license.
 DATA ls_iv_trade_license LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_trade_license.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_hist LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_IMP_EXP_TYPE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_imp_exp_type ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_imp_exp_type INTO ls_iv_imp_exp_type INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_imp_exp_type = ls_iv_imp_exp_type-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_PERMIT'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_permit ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_permit INTO ls_iv_permit INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_permit = ls_iv_permit-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_REGISTERED_EMIRATES'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_registered_emirates ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_registered_emirates INTO ls_iv_registered_emirates INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_registered_emirates = ls_iv_registered_emirates-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_TRADE_LICENSE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_trade_license ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_trade_license INTO ls_iv_trade_license INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_trade_license = ls_iv_trade_license-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZFE_CJ_CHEMICALS_HIST'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_imp_exp_type        = iv_imp_exp_type
           iv_permit              = iv_permit
           iv_registered_emirates = iv_registered_emirates
           iv_trade_license       = iv_trade_license
         IMPORTING
           et_hist                = et_hist
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure         = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS                 = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_imp_exp_type        = iv_imp_exp_type
       iv_permit              = iv_permit
       iv_registered_emirates = iv_registered_emirates
       iv_trade_license       = iv_trade_license
     IMPORTING
       et_hist                = et_hist
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure         = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure  = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                 = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_hist ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_hist INTO ls_et_hist
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_hist-mandt = ls_et_hist-mandt.
   ls_gw_et_hist-impexptype = ls_et_hist-impexptype.
   ls_gw_et_hist-permit = ls_et_hist-permit.
   ls_gw_et_hist-trade_license = ls_et_hist-trade_license.
   ls_gw_et_hist-registered_emirates = ls_et_hist-registered_emirates.
   ls_gw_et_hist-hs_code = ls_et_hist-hs_code.
   ls_gw_et_hist-cheminal_name = ls_et_hist-cheminal_name.
   ls_gw_et_hist-material_name = ls_et_hist-material_name.
   ls_gw_et_hist-chemical_formula = ls_et_hist-chemical_formula.
   ls_gw_et_hist-cas_no = ls_et_hist-cas_no.
   ls_gw_et_hist-unit = ls_et_hist-unit.
   ls_gw_et_hist-packaging = ls_et_hist-packaging.
   ls_gw_et_hist-country_origin = ls_et_hist-country_origin.
   ls_gw_et_hist-point_of_entrance = ls_et_hist-point_of_entrance.
   ls_gw_et_hist-transport_company = ls_et_hist-transport_company.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_hist TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_hist.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.

  method CHEMICALHISTORYS_UPDATE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'CHEMICALHISTORYS_UPDATE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.

* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->CREATEPAYMENTREQ_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING

* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TT_CREATEPAYMENTREQUEST
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method CREATEPAYMENTREQ_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA ev_case_stat TYPE string.
 DATA ev_case TYPE string.
 DATA ev_appl_no TYPE string.
 DATA iv_type TYPE string.
 DATA iv_sub_type TYPE string.
 DATA iv_note TYPE string.
 DATA iv_category TYPE string.
 DATA iv_amount TYPE zif_zfe_cj_create_payment_requ=>kbetr.
 DATA iv_company TYPE zif_zfe_cj_create_payment_requ=>bu_partner.
 DATA et_message  TYPE zif_zfe_cj_create_payment_requ=>bapiret2_t.
 DATA ls_et_message  TYPE LINE OF zif_zfe_cj_create_payment_requ=>bapiret2_t.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_type LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_type.
 DATA ls_iv_type LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_type.
 DATA lr_iv_sub_type LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_sub_type.
 DATA ls_iv_sub_type LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_sub_type.
 DATA lr_iv_note LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_note.
 DATA ls_iv_note LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_note.
 DATA lr_iv_company LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_company.
 DATA ls_iv_company LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_company.
 DATA lr_iv_category LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_category.
 DATA ls_iv_category LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_category.
 DATA lr_iv_amount LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_amount.
 DATA ls_iv_amount LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_amount.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_message LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_TYPE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_type ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_type INTO ls_iv_type INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_type = ls_iv_type-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_SUB_TYPE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_sub_type ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_sub_type INTO ls_iv_sub_type INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_sub_type = ls_iv_sub_type-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_NOTE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_note ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_note INTO ls_iv_note INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_note = ls_iv_note-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_COMPANY'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_company ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_company INTO ls_iv_company INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_company = ls_iv_company-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_CATEGORY'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_category ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_category INTO ls_iv_category INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_category = ls_iv_category-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_AMOUNT'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_amount ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_amount INTO ls_iv_amount INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_amount = ls_iv_amount-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).

         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING

     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZFE_CJ_CREATE_PAYMENT_REQUEST'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_type        = iv_type
           iv_sub_type    = iv_sub_type
           iv_note        = iv_note
           iv_company     = iv_company
           iv_category    = iv_category
           iv_amount      = iv_amount
         IMPORTING
           ev_case_stat   = ev_case_stat
           ev_case        = ev_case
           ev_appl_no     = ev_appl_no
           et_message     = et_message
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS         = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_type               = iv_type
       iv_sub_type           = iv_sub_type
       iv_note               = iv_note
       iv_company            = iv_company
       iv_category           = iv_category
       iv_amount             = iv_amount
     IMPORTING
       ev_case_stat          = ev_case_stat
       ev_case               = ev_case
       ev_appl_no            = ev_appl_no
       et_message            = et_message
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_message ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_message INTO ls_et_message
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_message-system = ls_et_message-system.
   ls_gw_et_message-field = ls_et_message-field.
   ls_gw_et_message-row = ls_et_message-row.
   ls_gw_et_message-parameter = ls_et_message-parameter.
   ls_gw_et_message-message_v4 = ls_et_message-message_v4.
   ls_gw_et_message-message_v3 = ls_et_message-message_v3.
   ls_gw_et_message-message_v2 = ls_et_message-message_v2.
   ls_gw_et_message-message_v1 = ls_et_message-message_v1.
   ls_gw_et_message-log_msg_no = ls_et_message-log_msg_no.
   ls_gw_et_message-log_no = ls_et_message-log_no.
   ls_gw_et_message-message = ls_et_message-message.
   ls_gw_et_message-number = ls_et_message-number.
   ls_gw_et_message-id = ls_et_message-id.
   ls_gw_et_message-type = ls_et_message-type.
   ls_gw_et_message-ev_case_stat = ev_case_stat.
   ls_gw_et_message-ev_case = ev_case.
   ls_gw_et_message-ev_appl_no = ev_appl_no.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_message TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_message.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.

* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->DOCUMENTV2SET_UPDATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_U(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_DOCUMENTV2
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method DOCUMENTV2SET_UPDATE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'DOCUMENTV2SET_UPDATE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->IF_SADL_GW_DPC_UTIL~GET_DPC
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [<-()] RO_DPC                         TYPE REF TO IF_SADL_GW_DPC
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method IF_SADL_GW_DPC_UTIL~GET_DPC.
    TYPES ty_zc_ega_fc_with_texts_1 TYPE zc_ega_fc_with_texts ##NEEDED. " reference for where-used list
    TYPES ty_zdt_srv_defaults_2 TYPE zdt_srv_defaults ##NEEDED. " reference for where-used list

    DATA(lv_sadl_xml) =
               |<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-16"?>| &
               |<sadl:definition xmlns:sadl="http://sap.com/sap.nw.f.sadl" syntaxVersion="V2" >| &
               | <sadl:dataSource type="CDS" name="FieldControlSet" binding="ZC_EGA_FC_WITH_TEXTS" />| &
               | <sadl:dataSource type="DDIC" name="ServiceDefaultsSet" binding="ZDT_SRV_DEFAULTS" />| &
               |<sadl:resultSet>| &
               |<sadl:structure name="ServiceDefaultsSet" dataSource="ServiceDefaultsSet" maxEditMode="RO" >| &
               | <sadl:query name="EntitySetDefault">| &
               | </sadl:query>| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="CASE_TYPE" binding="CASE_TYPE" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="TRUE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="SERV_TYPE" binding="SERV_TYPE" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="TRUE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="DEFAULT_TYPE" binding="DEFAULT_TYPE" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="TRUE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="DEFAULT_VALUE" binding="DEFAULT_VALUE" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="FALSE" />| &
               |</sadl:structure>| &
               |<sadl:structure name="FieldControlSet" dataSource="FieldControlSet" maxEditMode="RO" >| &
               | <sadl:query name="EntitySetDefault">| &
               | </sadl:query>| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="TEXT" binding="TEXT" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="FALSE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="SECIONNAME" binding="SECIONNAME" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="TRUE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="FIELDNAME" binding="FIELDNAME" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="TRUE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="READ_ONLY" binding="READ_ONLY" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="FALSE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="CASE_TYPE" binding="CASE_TYPE" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="TRUE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="MANDATORY" binding="MANDATORY" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="FALSE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="HIDDEN" binding="HIDDEN" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="FALSE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="OTR_ALIAS" binding="OTR_ALIAS" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="FALSE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="IS_RESULTPAGE" binding="IS_RESULTPAGE" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="FALSE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="LANGU" binding="LANGU" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="FALSE" />| &
               | <sadl:attribute name="SUBMISSION_TYPE" binding="SUBMISSION_TYPE" isOutput="TRUE" isKey="TRUE" />| &
               |</sadl:structure>| &
               |</sadl:resultSet>| &
               |</sadl:definition>| .
    ro_dpc = cl_sadl_gw_dpc_factory=>create_for_sadl( iv_sadl_xml   = lv_sadl_xml
               iv_timestamp         = 20250829041623
               iv_uuid              = 'ZEGA_FW_FND'
               io_query_control     = me
               io_extension_control = me
               io_context           = me->mo_context ).
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->PARTNERRELATIONS_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_PARTNERRELATIONS
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PARTNERRELATIONS_GET_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'PARTNERRELATIONS_GET_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->PARTNERRELATIONS_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TT_PARTNERRELATIONS
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PARTNERRELATIONS_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA iv_partner TYPE zif_zfe_cj_get_partner_relatio=>bu_partner.
 DATA et_partners  TYPE zif_zfe_cj_get_partner_relatio=>ztt_burs_joel_ovr_tc.
 DATA ls_et_partners  TYPE LINE OF zif_zfe_cj_get_partner_relatio=>ztt_burs_joel_ovr_tc.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_partner LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_partner.
 DATA ls_iv_partner LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_partner.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_partners LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_PARTNER'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_partner ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_partner INTO ls_iv_partner INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_partner = ls_iv_partner-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZFE_CJ_GET_PARTNER_RELATIONS'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_partner     = iv_partner
         IMPORTING
           et_partners    = et_partners
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS         = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_partner            = iv_partner
     IMPORTING
       et_partners           = et_partners
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_partners ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_partners INTO ls_et_partners
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_partners-bup_parnr = ls_et_partners-bup_parnr.
   ls_gw_et_partners-relshp_text = ls_et_partners-relshp_text.
   ls_gw_et_partners-smtp_address = ls_et_partners-smtp_address.
   ls_gw_et_partners-fax_extens = ls_et_partners-fax_extens.
   ls_gw_et_partners-fax_number = ls_et_partners-fax_number.
   ls_gw_et_partners-tel_extens = ls_et_partners-tel_extens.
   ls_gw_et_partners-tel_number = ls_et_partners-tel_number.
   ls_gw_et_partners-pavip_txt = ls_et_partners-pavip_txt.
   ls_gw_et_partners-pavip = ls_et_partners-pavip.
   ls_gw_et_partners-abtnr_txt = ls_et_partners-abtnr_txt.
   ls_gw_et_partners-abtnr = ls_et_partners-abtnr.
   ls_gw_et_partners-pafkt_txt = ls_et_partners-pafkt_txt.
   ls_gw_et_partners-pafkt = ls_et_partners-pafkt.
   ls_gw_et_partners-xdfrel = ls_et_partners-xdfrel.
   ls_gw_et_partners-bpext = ls_et_partners-bpext.
   ls_gw_et_partners-birthdt = ls_et_partners-birthdt.
   ls_gw_et_partners-bu_sort2 = ls_et_partners-bu_sort2.
   ls_gw_et_partners-bu_sort1 = ls_et_partners-bu_sort1.
   ls_gw_et_partners-mc_name2 = ls_et_partners-mc_name2.
   ls_gw_et_partners-mc_name1 = ls_et_partners-mc_name1.
   ls_gw_et_partners-house_num1 = ls_et_partners-house_num1.
   ls_gw_et_partners-street = ls_et_partners-street.
   ls_gw_et_partners-city1 = ls_et_partners-city1.
   ls_gw_et_partners-post_code1 = ls_et_partners-post_code1.
   ls_gw_et_partners-country = ls_et_partners-country.
   ls_gw_et_partners-name2 = ls_et_partners-name2.
   ls_gw_et_partners-name1 = ls_et_partners-name1.
   ls_gw_et_partners-dftval_text = ls_et_partners-dftval_text.
   ls_gw_et_partners-valid_to_date = ls_et_partners-valid_to_date.
   ls_gw_et_partners-valid_from_date = ls_et_partners-valid_from_date.
   ls_gw_et_partners-partner_to_description = ls_et_partners-partner_to_description.
   ls_gw_et_partners-addr_type_text = ls_et_partners-addr_type_text.
   ls_gw_et_partners-partner_to_number = ls_et_partners-partner_to_number.
   ls_gw_et_partners-directed_type_title = ls_et_partners-directed_type_title.
   ls_gw_et_partners-index = ls_et_partners-index.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_partners TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_partners.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_U(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->PASSPORTTYPESSET_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_PASSPORTTYPES
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PASSPORTTYPESSET_GET_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'PASSPORTTYPESSET_GET_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->PASSPORTTYPESSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TT_PASSPORTTYPES
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PASSPORTTYPESSET_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA iv_app TYPE string.
 DATA et_values  TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_passport_typ=>wdy_key_value_list.
 DATA ls_et_values  TYPE LINE OF zif_zfe_cj_search_passport_typ=>wdy_key_value_list.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_app LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_app.
 DATA ls_iv_app LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_app.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_values LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_APP'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_app ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_app INTO ls_iv_app INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_app = ls_iv_app-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZFE_CJ_SEARCH_PASSPORT_TYPES'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_app         = iv_app
         IMPORTING
           et_values      = et_values
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS         = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_app                = iv_app
     IMPORTING
       et_values             = et_values
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_values ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_values INTO ls_et_values
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_values-key = ls_et_values-key.
   ls_gw_et_values-value = ls_et_values-value.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_values TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_values.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->PASSPORTTYPESSET_UPDATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_U(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_PASSPORTTYPES
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PASSPORTTYPESSET_UPDATE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'PASSPORTTYPESSET_UPDATE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->PAYMENTSERVICESE_CREATE_ENTITY

* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_PAYMENTSERVICE
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION

  method PAYMENTSERVICESE_CREATE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc

      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->PAYMENTSERVICESE_DELETE_ENTITY

      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'PAYMENTSERVICESE_DELETE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->PAYMENTSERVICESE_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_PAYMENTSERVICE
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PAYMENTSERVICESE_GET_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'PAYMENTSERVICESE_GET_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->PAYMENTSERVICESE_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TT_PAYMENTSERVICE
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PAYMENTSERVICESE_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA iv_dummy TYPE string.
 DATA et_services  TYPE zif_zfe_cj_payment_service_t1=>wdr_param_tree.
 DATA ls_et_services  TYPE LINE OF zif_zfe_cj_payment_service_t1=>wdr_param_tree.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_dummy LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_dummy.
 DATA ls_iv_dummy LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_dummy.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_services LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_DUMMY'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_dummy ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_dummy INTO ls_iv_dummy INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_dummy = ls_iv_dummy-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZFE_CJ_PAYMENT_SERVICE_TYPES'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_dummy       = iv_dummy
         IMPORTING
           et_services    = et_services
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS         = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_dummy              = iv_dummy
     IMPORTING
       et_services           = et_services
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_services ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_services INTO ls_et_services
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_services-value = ls_et_services-value.
   ls_gw_et_services-childid = ls_et_services-childid.
   ls_gw_et_services-parentid = ls_et_services-parentid.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_services TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_services.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.

* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)

      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented

  endmethod.

      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION

  method READLISTVALUESSE_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA iv_code TYPE string.
 DATA et_values  TYPE zif_zfe_cj_read_list2=>wdy_key_value_list.
 DATA ls_et_values  TYPE LINE OF zif_zfe_cj_read_list2=>wdy_key_value_list.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_code LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_code.
 DATA ls_iv_code LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_code.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_values LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_CODE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_code ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_code INTO ls_iv_code INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_code = ls_iv_code-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZFE_CJ_READ_LIST'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_code        = iv_code
         IMPORTING
           et_values      = et_values
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS         = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_code               = iv_code
     IMPORTING
       et_values             = et_values
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_values ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_values INTO ls_et_values
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_values-value = ls_et_values-value.
   ls_gw_et_values-key = ls_et_values-key.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_values TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_values.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.
