      method = 'SEARCHBPSET_CREATE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SEARCHBPSET_DELETE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_D(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SEARCHBPSET_DELETE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'SEARCHBPSET_DELETE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SEARCHBPSET_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_SEARCHBP
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SEARCHBPSET_GET_ENTITY.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA iv_app TYPE string.
 DATA ev_date_of_birth TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>bu_birthdt.
 DATA ev_email TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>ad_smtpadr.
 DATA ev_id_number TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>bu_id_number.
 DATA ev_name TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>bu_name1tx.
 DATA ev_nationality TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>natio50.
 DATA ev_nationality_key TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>bu_natio.
 DATA ev_partner TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>partner.
 DATA ev_passport TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>bu_id_number.
 DATA ev_phone TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>farp_mobile.
 DATA iv_idnumber TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>bu_id_number.
 DATA iv_type TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>bu_id_type.
 DATA ev_message TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_bp_by_id=>bapiret2-message.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE er_entity.
 DATA lv_source_entity_set_name TYPE string.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get key table information - for direct call
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values = ls_converted_keys ).

* Maps key fields to function module parameters

 lv_source_entity_set_name = io_tech_request_context->get_source_entity_set_name( ).

 IF lv_source_entity_set_name = 'SearchBPSet' AND
    lv_source_entity_set_name NE io_tech_request_context->get_entity_set_name( ).

   io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING es_key_values = ls_converted_keys ).

 ENDIF.

 iv_app = ls_converted_keys-iv_app.
 iv_idnumber = ls_converted_keys-iv_idnumber.
 iv_type = ls_converted_keys-iv_type.
* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZFE_CJ_SEARCH_BP_BY_ID'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_app             = iv_app
           iv_idnumber        = iv_idnumber
           iv_type            = iv_type
         IMPORTING
           ev_date_of_birth   = ev_date_of_birth
           ev_nationality_key = ev_nationality_key
           ev_passport        = ev_passport
           ev_nationality     = ev_nationality
           ev_email           = ev_email
           ev_id_number       = ev_id_number
           ev_message         = ev_message
           ev_name            = ev_name
           ev_partner         = ev_partner
           ev_phone           = ev_phone
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure     = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS             = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_app                = iv_app
       iv_idnumber           = iv_idnumber
       iv_type               = iv_type
     IMPORTING
       ev_date_of_birth      = ev_date_of_birth
       ev_nationality_key    = ev_nationality_key
       ev_passport           = ev_passport
       ev_nationality        = ev_nationality
       ev_email              = ev_email
       ev_id_number          = ev_id_number
       ev_message            = ev_message
       ev_name               = ev_name
       ev_partner            = ev_partner
       ev_phone              = ev_phone
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response structure

 er_entity-iv_app = iv_app.
 er_entity-iv_idnumber = iv_idnumber.
 er_entity-iv_type = iv_type.
 er_entity-ev_date_of_birth = ev_date_of_birth.
 er_entity-ev_nationality_key = ev_nationality_key.
 er_entity-ev_passport = ev_passport.
 er_entity-ev_nationality = ev_nationality.
 er_entity-ev_email = ev_email.
 er_entity-ev_id_number = ev_id_number.
 er_entity-ev_message = ev_message.
 er_entity-ev_name = ev_name.
 er_entity-ev_partner = ev_partner.
 er_entity-ev_phone = ev_phone.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SEARCHBPSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING

  endmethod.

* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)

  method SEARCHBPSET_UPDATE_ENTITY.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SEARCHCOMPANYSET_CREATE_ENTITY

* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_C(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_SEARCHCOMPANY
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SEARCHCOMPANYSET_CREATE_ENTITY.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_D(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION

  method SEARCHCOMPANYSET_DELETE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'SEARCHCOMPANYSET_DELETE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SEARCHCOMPANYSET_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_SEARCHCOMPANY
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SEARCHCOMPANYSET_GET_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'SEARCHCOMPANYSET_GET_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SEARCHCOMPANYSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TT_SEARCHCOMPANY
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SEARCHCOMPANYSET_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA iv_search TYPE string.
 DATA et_companies  TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_company=>ztt_cj_companies.
 DATA ls_et_companies  TYPE LINE OF zif_zfe_cj_search_company=>ztt_cj_companies.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_search LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_search.
 DATA ls_iv_search LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_search.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_companies LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_SEARCH'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_search ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_search INTO ls_iv_search INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_search = ls_iv_search-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZFE_CJ_SEARCH_COMPANY'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_search      = iv_search
         IMPORTING
           et_companies   = et_companies
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS         = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_search             = iv_search
     IMPORTING
       et_companies          = et_companies
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_companies ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_companies INTO ls_et_companies
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_companies-phone = ls_et_companies-phone.
   ls_gw_et_companies-email = ls_et_companies-email.
   ls_gw_et_companies-emirate = ls_et_companies-emirate.
   ls_gw_et_companies-trade_license = ls_et_companies-trade_license.
   ls_gw_et_companies-name_en = ls_et_companies-name_en.
   ls_gw_et_companies-name_ar = ls_et_companies-name_ar.
   ls_gw_et_companies-partner = ls_et_companies-partner.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_companies TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_companies.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SEARCHCOMPANYSET_UPDATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR

* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH

* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SEARCHCOMPANYSET_UPDATE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc

      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented

  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SEARCHWITHPARAME_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_SEARCHWITHPARAMETER
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SEARCHWITHPARAME_GET_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'SEARCHWITHPARAME_GET_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SEARCHWITHPARAME_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TT_SEARCHWITHPARAMETER
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SEARCHWITHPARAME_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA ev_title TYPE string.
 DATA ev_name TYPE string.
 DATA iv_value TYPE string.
 DATA iv_method TYPE string.
 DATA iv_label TYPE string.
 DATA message TYPE string.
 DATA iv_additional_select TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_field_by_p1=>flag.
 DATA et_table_data  TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_field_by_p1=>/qnv/sb_tabl_def_tt.
 DATA return  TYPE zif_zfe_cj_search_field_by_p1=>bapiret2_t.
 DATA ls_et_table_data  TYPE LINE OF zif_zfe_cj_search_field_by_p1=>/qnv/sb_tabl_def_tt.
 DATA ls_return  TYPE LINE OF zif_zfe_cj_search_field_by_p1=>bapiret2_t.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_value LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_value.
 DATA ls_iv_value LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_value.
 DATA lr_iv_method LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_method.
 DATA ls_iv_method LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_method.
 DATA lr_iv_label LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_label.
 DATA ls_iv_label LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_label.
 DATA lr_iv_additional_select LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_additional_select.
 DATA ls_iv_additional_select LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_additional_select.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_table_data LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_VALUE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_value ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_value INTO ls_iv_value INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_value = ls_iv_value-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_METHOD'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_method ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_method INTO ls_iv_method INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_method = ls_iv_method-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_LABEL'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_label ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_label INTO ls_iv_label INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_label = ls_iv_label-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_ADDITIONAL_SELECT'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_additional_select ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_additional_select INTO ls_iv_additional_select INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_additional_select = ls_iv_additional_select-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZFE_CJ_SEARCH_FIELD_BY_PARAM'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_value             = iv_value
           iv_method            = iv_method
           iv_label             = iv_label
           iv_additional_select = iv_additional_select
         IMPORTING
           return               = return
           ev_title             = ev_title
           ev_name              = ev_name
           et_table_data        = et_table_data
           message              = message
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure       = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS               = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_value              = iv_value
       iv_method             = iv_method
       iv_label              = iv_label
       iv_additional_select  = iv_additional_select
     IMPORTING
       return                = return
       ev_title              = ev_title
       ev_name               = ev_name
       et_table_data         = et_table_data
       message               = message
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

 IF return IS NOT INITIAL.
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_save_log(
     EXPORTING
       iv_entity_type = iv_entity_name
       it_return      = return
       it_key_tab     = it_key_tab ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_table_data ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_table_data INTO ls_et_table_data
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column30 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column30.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column29 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column29.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column28 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column28.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column27 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column27.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column26 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column26.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column25 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column25.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column24 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column24.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column23 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column23.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column22 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column22.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column21 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column21.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column20 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column20.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column19 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column19.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column18 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column18.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column17 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column17.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column16 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column16.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column15 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column15.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column14 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column14.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column13 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column13.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column12 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column12.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column11 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column11.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column10 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column10.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column9 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column9.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column8 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column8.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column7 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column7.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column6 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column6.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column5 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column5.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column4 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column4.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column3 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column3.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column2 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column2.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_column1 = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_column1.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-sequence = ls_et_table_data-sequence.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ui_table_name = ls_et_table_data-ui_table_name.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-categoryname = ls_et_table_data-categoryname.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-screen_name = ls_et_table_data-screen_name.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-mandt = ls_et_table_data-mandt.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ev_title = ev_title.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-ev_name = ev_name.
   ls_gw_et_table_data-message = message.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_table_data TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_table_data.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SEARCHWITHPARAME_UPDATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_U(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_SEARCHWITHPARAMETER
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SEARCHWITHPARAME_UPDATE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'SEARCHWITHPARAME_UPDATE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SERVICEDEFAULTSS_CREATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_C(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_MPC=>TS_SERVICEDEFAULTS
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SERVICEDEFAULTSS_CREATE_ENTITY.
    if_sadl_gw_dpc_util~get_dpc( )->create_entity( EXPORTING io_data_provider        = io_data_provider
                                                             io_tech_request_context = io_tech_request_context
                                                   IMPORTING es_data                 = er_entity ).
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SERVICEDEFAULTSS_DELETE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_D(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method SERVICEDEFAULTSS_DELETE_ENTITY.
    if_sadl_gw_dpc_util~get_dpc( )->delete_entity( io_tech_request_context ).
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_FW_FND_DPC->SERVICEDEFAULTSS_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)

* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>

    if_sadl_gw_dpc_util~get_dpc( )->get_entity( EXPORTING io_tech_request_context = io_tech_request_context
                                                IMPORTING es_data                 = er_entity ).
